"chunk_index": 0, "content": "\"\"\"SQLite storage backend (default).\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional\n\nfrom scribe_mcp.storage.base import StorageBackend\nfrom scribe_mcp.storage.models import (\n    ProjectRecord, DevPlanRecord, PhaseRecord, MilestoneRecord,\n    BenchmarkRecord, ChecklistRecord, PerformanceMetricsRecord,\n    DocumentSectionRecord, CustomTemplateRecord, DocumentChangeRecord, SyncStatusRecord\n)\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.time import format_utc, utcnow\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.search import message_matches\n\nSQLITE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 30\nSQLITE_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000\n\n\nclass SQLiteStorage(StorageBackend):\n    \"\"\"SQLite-backed persistence with lazy connections.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, db_path: Path   str) -> None:\n        self._path = Path(db_path).expanduser()\n        self._init_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._write_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._initialised = False\n\n    async def setup(self) -> None:\n        await self._initialise()\n\n    async def close(self) -> None:  # pragma: no cover - nothing persistent to close\n        # Connections are short-lived; nothing to do.\n        return None\n\n    async def upsert_project(\n        self,\n        *,\n        name: str,\n        repo_root: str,\n        progress_log_path: str,\n    ) -> ProjectRecord:\n        await self._initialise()\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            await self._execute(\n            \"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO scribe_projects (name, repo_root, progress_log_path)\n            VALUES (?, ?, ?)\n            ON CONFLICT(name)\n            DO UPDATE SET repo_root = excluded.repo_root,\n                          progress_log_path = excluded.progress_log_path;\n            \"\"\",\n            (name, repo_root, progress_log_path),\n        )\n        row = await self._fetchone(\n            \"\"\"\n            SELECT id, name, repo_root, progress_log_path\n            FROM scribe_projects\n            WHERE name = ?;\n            \"\"\",\n            (name,),\n        )\n        return ProjectRecord(\n            id=row[\"id\"],\n            name=row[\"name\"],\n            repo_root=row[\"repo_root\"],\n            progress_log_path=row[\"progress_log_path\"],\n        )\n\n    async def fetch_project(self, name: str) -> Optional[ProjectRecord]:\n        await self._initialise()\n        row = await self._fetchone(\n            \"\"\"\n            SELECT id, name, repo_root, progress_log_path\n            FROM scribe_projects\n            WHERE name = ?;\n            \"\"\",\n            (name,),\n        )\n        if not row:\n            return None\n        return ProjectRecord(\n            id=row[\"id\"],\n            name=row[\"name\"],\n            repo_root=row[\"repo_root\"],\n            progress_log_path=row[\"progress_log_path\"],\n        )\n\n    async def list_projects(self) -> List[ProjectRecord]:\n        await self._initialise()\n        rows = await self._fetchall(\n            \"\"\"\n            SELECT id, name, repo_root, progress_log_path\n            FROM scribe_projects\n            ORDER BY name;\n            \"\"\"\n        )\n        records: List[ProjectRecord] = []\n        for row in rows:\n            records.append(\n                ProjectRecord(\n                    id=row[\"id\"],\n                    name=row[\"name\"],\n                    repo_root=row[\"repo_root\"],\n                    progress_log_path=row[\"progress_log_path\"],\n                )\n            )\n        return records\n\n    async def delete_project(self, name: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Delete a project and all associated data with proper cascade handling.\"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n\n        # First check if project exists\n        project = await self.fetch_project(name)\n        if not project:\n            return False\n\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            # Delete project and all related data using proper cascade order\n            # SQLite foreign key constraints should handle most of this automatically,\n            # but we'll be explicit for safety and clarity\n\n            # Delete agent project associations\n            await self._execute(\n                \"DELETE FROM agent_projects WHERE project_name = ?;\",\n                (name,),\n            )\n\n            # Delete global log entries for this project (if they exist)\n            # Note: global_log_entries uses project_id, but table is currently empty\n            # await self._execute(\n            #     \"DELETE FROM global_log_entries WHERE project_id = ?;\",\n            #     (name,),\n            # )\n\n            # Due to foreign key constraints with ON DELETE CASCADE,\n            # deleting the project should automatically clean up:\n            # - scribe_entries\n            # - dev_plans -> phases -> milestones\n            # - benchmarks, checklists, performance_metrics\n            # - documents -> document_sections, document_changes\n            # - custom_templates, sync_status\n            # - scribe_metrics\n\n            # Delete the main project record (this will cascade to related tables)\n            await self._execute(\n                \"DELETE FROM scribe_projects WHERE name = ?;\",\n                (name,),\n            )\n\n            # Verify deletion\n            remaining = await self._fetchone(\n                \"SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM scribe_projects WHERE name = ?;\",\n                (name,),\n            )\n\n            return remaining[\"count\"] == 0\n\n    async def insert_entry(\n        self,\n        *,\n        entry_id: str,\n        project: ProjectRecord,\n        ts: datetime,\n        emoji: str,\n        agent: Optional[str],\n        message: str,\n        meta: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],\n        raw_line: str,\n        sha256: str,\n    ) -> None:\n        await self._initialise()\n        ts_iso = ts.isoformat()\n        meta_json = json.dumps(meta or {}, sort_keys=True)\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            await self._execute(\n                \"\"\"\n                INSERT OR IGNORE INTO scribe_entries\n                    (id, project_id, ts, emoji, agent, message, meta, raw_line, sha256, ts_iso)\n                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);\n                \"\"\",\n                (\n                    entry_id,\n                    project.id,\n                    format_utc(ts),\n                    emoji,\n                    agent,\n                    message,\n                    meta_json,\n                    raw_line,\n                    sha256,\n                    ts_iso,\n                ),\n            )\n", "line_end": 200, "content": "def parse_log_line(line: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Parse a canonical Scribe log line into structured fields.\"\"\"\n    match = LOG_LINE_PATTERN.match(line.strip())\n    if not match:\n        return None\n\n    timestamp = match.group(\"timestamp\")\n    emoji = match.group(\"emoji\")\n    agent = match.group(\"agent\")\n    message = match.group(\"message\")\n\n    # Filter out template/example entries\n    if _is_template_entry(timestamp, emoji, agent, message):\n        return None\n\n    meta_text = match.group(\"meta\")\n    meta: Dict[str, str] = {}\n    if meta_text:\n        for chunk in meta_text.split(\";\"):\n            key, value = _split_meta_chunk(chunk)\n            if key:\n                meta[key] = value\n    return {\n        \"ts\": timestamp,\n        \"emoji\": emoji,\n        \"agent\": agent,\n        \"project\": match.group(\"project\"),\n        \"message\": message,\n        \"meta\": meta,\n        \"raw_line\": line.strip(),\n    }\n\n\ndef _split_meta_chunk(chunk: str) -> tuple[str, str]:\n    piece = chunk.strip()\n    if not piece:\n        return \"\", \"\"\n    if \"=\" not in piece:\n        return piece, \"\"\n    key, value = piece.split(\"=\", 1)\n    return key.strip(), value.strip()\n\n\nasync def read_all_lines(path: Path) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Read the entire file (or empty list on missing) without blocking the loop.\"\"\"\n    return await asyncio.to_thread(_read_lines, path)\n\n\ndef _read_lines(path: Path) -> List[str]:\n    try:\n        with path.open(\"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n            return [line.rstrip(\"\\n\") for line in handle]\n    except FileNotFoundError:\n", "line_end": 80, "line_start": 28}, "frontmatter": {}, match.group(\\\"agent\\\")\\n    message = match.group(\\\"message\\\")\\n\\n    # Filter out template/example entries\\n    if _is_template_entry(timestamp, emoji, agent, message):\\n        return None\\n\\n    meta_text = match.group(\\\"meta\\\")\\n    meta: Dict[str, str] = {}\\n    if meta_text:\\n        for chunk in meta_text.split(\\\";\\\"):\\n            key, value = _split_meta_chunk(chunk)\\n            if key:\\n                meta[key] = value\\n    return {\\n        \\\"ts\\\": timestamp,\\n        \\\"emoji\\\": emoji,\\n        \\\"agent\\\": agent,\\n \\\"\\\"\\n    key, value = piece.split(\\\"=\\\", 1)\\n    return key.strip(), value.strip()\\n\\n\\nasync def read_all_lines(path: Path) -> List[str]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Read the entire file (or empty list on missing) without blocking the loop.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return await asyncio.to_thread(_read_lines, path)\\n\\n\\ndef _read_lines(path: Path) -> List[str]:\\n    try:\\n        with Fallback: return original response with healing info\n        if healing_applied:\n            response[\"parameter_healing\"] = {\n                \"applied\": True,\n                \"messages\": healing_messages,\n                \"original_parameters\": {\"n\": healed_params[\"n\"], \"page\": healed_params[\"page\"], \"page_size\": healed_params[\"page_size\"]},\n                \"token_budget_error\": str(token_error)\n            }\n\n", "line_end": 480, "line_start": 400}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/tools/read_recent.py", "byte_size": 21591, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 3, "line_count": 537, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "tools/read_recent.py", "sha256": "49ba249162e6732797d191ca70972f9beb0f5d68acc3c035a7a586a989270175"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T04:56:46.316696Z; tool=read_file; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-03 04:57:04 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunks": [{"byte_end": 5512, "byte_start": 0, "chunk_index": 0, "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nEntryLimitManager - Intelligent entry limiting based on priority and display mode.\n\nReplaces TokenBudgetManager's blind truncation with count-based limits\nthat preserve complete entries and prioritize important content.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple\n\n\nclass EntryLimitManager:\n    \"\"\"Intelligent entry limiting based on priority and display mode.\n\n    Replaces TokenBudgetManager's blind truncation with count-based limits\n    that preserve complete entries and prioritize important content.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Default limits per display mode\n    MODE_LIMITS = {\n        \"summary\": 50,\n        \"full\": 10,\n        \"expandable\": 50,\n        \"structured\": 100,\n        \"compact\": 200,\n        \"readable\": 50,  # Same as summary\n    }\n\n    # Priority sort order (lower = higher priority)\n    PRIORITY_ORDER = {\n        \"critical\": 0,\n        \"high\": 1,\n        \"medium\": 2,\n        \"low\": 3,\n    }\n\n    def limit_entries(\n        self,\n        entries: List[Dict[str, Any]],\n        mode: str = \"summary\",\n        priority_filter: Optional[List[str]] = None,\n        max_entries: Optional[int] = None,\n        sort_by_priority: bool = True,\n    ) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], Dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Limit entries intelligently based on mode and priority.\n\n        Args:\n            entries: List of log entry dicts\n            mode: Display mode (summary, full, expandable, structured, compact, readable)\n            priority_filter: Only include entries with these priorities\n            max_entries: Override default limit for mode\n            sort_by_priority: If True, sort by priority first, then by time\n\n        Returns:\n            Tuple of (limited_entries, metadata_dict)\n\n        Metadata dict contains:\n            - total_available: Original entry count\n            - filtered_count: Count after priority filter\n            - returned_count: Final count returned\n            - entries_omitted: How many were cut\n            - mode: Display mode used\n            - limit_applied: The limit that was applied\n        \"\"\"\n        total_available = len(entries)\n\n        # Apply priority filter if provided\n        if priority_filter:\n            filtered_entries = self._filter_by_priority(entries, priority_filter)\n        else:\n            filtered_entries = entries.copy()\n\n        filtered_count = len(filtered_entries)\n\n        # Sort by priority if requested\n        if sort_by_priority:\n            # Sort by priority order (ASC), then by timestamp (DESC - newest first)\n            filtered_entries = sorted(\n                filtered_entries,\n                key=lambda e: (\n                    self.PRIORITY_ORDER.get(e.get(\"priority\", \"medium\"), 2),\n                    e.get(\"ts_iso\", \"\")\n                ),\n                reverse=False  # Priority ASC, but we need time DESC\n            )\n\n            # Fix time sorting: within each priority, sort newest first\n            # Group by priority and re-sort each group by time DESC\n            priority_groups: Dict[int, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}\n            for entry in filtered_entries:\n                priority = entry.get(\"priority\", \"medium\")\n                priority_order = self.PRIORITY_ORDER.get(priority, 2)\n                if priority_order not in priority_groups:\n                    priority_groups[priority_order] = []\n                priority_groups[priority_order].append(entry)\n\n            # Sort each group by timestamp DESC (newest first)\n            filtered_entries = []\n            for priority_order in sorted(priority_groups.keys()):\n                group = priority_groups[priority_order]\n                group_sorted = sorted(\n                    group,\n                    key=lambda e: e.get(\"ts_iso\", \"\"),\n                    reverse=True  # Newest first within priority group\n                )\n                filtered_entries.extend(group_sorted)\n\n        # Determine limit\n        limit = max_entries if max_entries is not None else self.MODE_LIMITS.get(mode, 50)\n\n        # Apply limit\n        limited_entries = filtered_entries[:limit]\n        returned_count = len(limited_entries)\n        entries_omitted = filtered_count - returned_count\n\n        # Build metadata\n        metadata = {\n            \"total_available\": total_available,\n            \"filtered_count\": filtered_count,\n            \"returned_count\": returned_count,\n            \"entries_omitted\": max(0, entries_omitted),\n            \"mode\": mode,\n            \"limit_applied\": limit,\n        }\n\n        return limited_entries, metadata\n\n    def _get_priority_sort_key(self, entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[int, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Generate sort key for priority-based ordering.\n        Returns (priority_order, timestamp) for proper sorting.\n\n        Note: This is a helper method. The actual sorting logic in limit_entries\n        handles the reversal for timestamp DESC within each priority group.\n        \"\"\"\n        priority = entry.get(\"priority\", \"medium\")\n        priority_order = self.PRIORITY_ORDER.get(priority, 2)  # Default to medium\n        ts_iso = entry.get(\"ts_iso\", \"\")\n        return (priority_order, ts_iso)\n\n    def _filter_by_priority(\n        self,\n        entries: List[Dict[str, Any]],\n        priority_filter: List[str]\n    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Filter entries to only include specified priorities.\"\"\"\n        return [\n            entry for entry in entries\n            if entry.get(\"priority\", \"medium\") in priority_filter\n        ]\n", "line_end": 151, "line_start": 1}], "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "chunk", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/utils/entry_limit.py", "byte_size": 5512, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 1, "line_count": 151, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "utils/entry_limit.py", "sha256": "ba138868c43aac25f6dd47f5a8270e6a3b0485ba75123dca424deda3a547a3fd"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T04:57:04.286959Z; tool=read_file; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

The actual sorting logic in limit_entries\\n        handles the reversal for timestamp DESC within each priority group.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        priority = entry.get(\\\"priority\\\", \\\"medium\\\")\\n        priority_order = self.PRIORITY_ORDER.get(priority, 2)  # Default to medium\\n        ts_iso = entry.get(\\\"ts_iso\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n        return (priority_order, ts_iso)\\n\\n    def _filter_by_priority(\\n        self,\\n        entries: List[Dict[str, Any]],\\n        priority_filter: List[str]\\n    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Filter entries to only include specified priorities.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return [\\n            entry for entry in entries\\n            if entry.get(\\\"priority\\\", \\\"medium\\\") in priority_filter\\n        ]\\n\", \"line_end\": 151, \"line_start\": 1}], \"frontmatter\": {}, \"frontmatter_byte_count\": 0, \"frontmatter_line_count\": 0, \"frontmatter_raw\": \"\", \"has_frontmatter\": false, \"mode\": \"chunk\", \"ok\": true, \"reminders\": [{\"category\": \"context\", \"context\": \"Entries:    Last: \", \"emoji\": \"\\ud83c\\udfaf\", \"level\": \"info\", \"message\": \"Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement\", \"score\": 3, \"tone\": \"neutral\"}], \"scan\": {\"absolute_path\": \"/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/utils/entry_limit.py\", \"byte_size\": 5512, \"encoding\": \"utf-8\", \"estimated_chunk_count\": 1, \"line_count\": 151, \"newline_type\": \"LF\", \"repo_relative_path\": \"utils/entry_limit.py\", \"sha256\": \"ba138868c43aac25f6dd47f5a8270e6a3b0485ba75123dca424deda3a547a3fd\"}}", "timestamp": "2026-01-03T04:57:04.286959Z", "tool": "read_file"}, "raw_line": "[\u2139\ufe0f] [2026-01-03 04:57:04 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file   format_requested=readable; response_data={\"chunks\": [{\"byte_end\": 5512, \"byte_start\": 0, \"chunk_index\": 0, \"content\": \"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nEntryLimitManager - Intelligent entry limiting based on priority and display mode.\\n\\nReplaces TokenBudgetManager's blind truncation with count-based limits\\nthat preserve complete entries and prioritize important content.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple\\n\\n\\nclass EntryLimitManager:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Intelligent entry limiting based on priority and display mode.\\n\\n    Replaces TokenBudgetManager's blind truncation with count-based limits\\n    that preserve complete entries and prioritize important content.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    # Default limits per display mode\\n    MODE_LIMITS = {\\n        \\\"summary\\\": 50,\\n        \\\"full\\\": 10,\\n        \\\"expandable\\\": 50,\\n        \\\"structured\\\": 100,\\n        \\\"compact\\\": 200,\\n        \\\"readable\\\": 50,  # Same as summary\\n    }\\n\\n    # Priority sort order (lower = higher priority)\\n    PRIORITY_ORDER = {\\n        \\\"critical\\\": 0,\\n        \\\"high\\\": 1,\\n        \\\"medium\\\": 2,\\n        \\\"low\\\": 3,\\n    }\\n\\n    def limit_entries(\\n        self,\\n        entries: List[Dict[str, Any]],\\n        mode: str = \\\"summary\\\",\\n        priority_filter: Optional[List[str]] = None,\\n        max_entries: Optional[int] = None,\\n        sort_by_priority: bool = True,\\n    ) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Limit entries intelligently based on mode and priority.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            entries: List of log entry dicts\\n            mode: Display mode (summary, full, expandable, structured, compact, readable)\\n            priority_filter: Only include entries with these priorities\\n            max_entries: Override default limit for mode\\n            sort_by_priority: If True, sort by priority first, then by time\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Tuple of (limited_entries, metadata_dict)\\n\\n        Metadata dict contains:\\n            - total_available: Original entry count\\n            - filtered_count: Count after priority filter\\n            - returned_count: Final count returned\\n            - entries_omitted: How many were cut\\n            - mode: Display mode used\\n            - limit_applied: The limit that was applied\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        total_available = len(entries)\\n\\n        # Apply priority filter if provided\\n        if priority_filter:\\n            filtered_entries = self._filter_by_priority(entries, priority_filter)\\n        else:\\n            filtered_entries = entries.copy()\\n\\n        filtered_count = len(filtered_entries)\\n\\n        # Sort by priority if requested\\n        if sort_by_priority:\\n            # Sort by priority order (ASC), then by timestamp (DESC - newest first)\\n            filtered_entries = sorted(\\n                filtered_entries,\\n                key=lambda e: (\\n                    self.PRIORITY_ORDER.get(e.get(\\\"priority\\\", \\\"medium\\\"), 2),\\n                    e.get(\\\"ts_iso\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n                ),\\n                reverse=False  # Priority ASC, but we need time DESC\\n            )\\n\\n            # Fix time sorting: within each priority, sort newest first\\n            # Group by priority and re-sort each group by time DESC\\n            priority_groups: Dict[int, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}\\n            for entry in filtered_entries:\\n                priority = entry.get(\\\"priority\\\", \\\"medium\\\")\\n                priority_order = self.PRIORITY_ORDER.get(priority, 2)\\n                if priority_order not in priority_groups:\\n                    priority_groups[priority_order] = []\\n                priority_groups[priority_order].append(entry)\\n\\n            # Sort each group by timestamp DESC (newest first)\\n            filtered_entries = []\\n            for priority_order in sorted(priority_groups.keys()):\\n                group = priority_groups[priority_order]\\n                group_sorted = sorted(\\n                    group,\\n                    key=lambda e: e.get(\\\"ts_iso\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n                    reverse=True  # Newest first within priority group\\n                )\\n                filtered_entries.extend(group_sorted)\\n\\n        # Determine limit\\n        limit = max_entries if max_entries is not None else self.MODE_LIMITS.get(mode, 50)\\n\\n        # Apply limit\\n        limited_entries = filtered_entries[:limit]\\n        returned_count = len(limited_entries)\\n        entries_omitted = filtered_count - returned_count\\n\\n        # Build metadata\\n        metadata = {\\n            \\\"total_available\\\": total_available,\\n            \\\"filtered_count\\\": filtered_count,\\n            \\\"returned_count\\\": returned_count,\\n            \\\"entries_omitted\\\": max(0, entries_omitted),\\n            \\\"mode\\\": mode,\\n            \\\"limit_applied\\\": limit,\\n        }\\n\\n        return limited_entries, metadata\\n\\n    def _get_priority_sort_key(self, entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[int, str]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Generate sort key for priority-based ordering.\\n        Returns (priority_order, timestamp) for proper sorting.\\n\\n        Note: This is a helper method. The actual sorting logic in limit_entries\\n        handles the reversal for timestamp DESC within each priority group.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        priority = entry.get(\\\"priority\\\", \\\"medium\\\")\\n        priority_order = self.PRIORITY_ORDER.get(priority, 2)  # Default to medium\\n        ts_iso = entry.get(\\\"ts_iso\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n        return (priority_order, ts_iso)\\n\\n    def _filter_by_priority(\\n        self,\\n        entries: List[Dict[str, Any]],\\n        priority_filter: List[str]\\n    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Filter entries to only include specified priorities.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return [\\n            entry for entry in entries\\n            if entry.get(\\\"priority\\\", \\\"medium\\\") in priority_filter\\n        ]\\n\", \"line_end\": 151, \"line_start\": 1}], \"frontmatter\": {}, \"frontmatter_byte_count\": 0, \"frontmatter_line_count\": 0, \"frontmatter_raw\": \"\", \"has_frontmatter\": false,
